Let's think of a more principled local optimizer: Use exact KKT conditions for the continuous problem. At optimum, h feasible and there exist a probability distribution λ over active lags such that gradient of weighted objective plus λ sum constraint is orthogonal to feasible directions (within bounds). Specifically for h_i interior (0,1), ∑_k λ_k ∂c_k/∂h_i = μ (constant), because sum constraint. For bounded, KKT with inequalities. We can implement active set policy: choose active lags weighted, solve dual? This might find exact local optimum.

For a given h, compute c. Let active set A = {d: c_d close to C}. We want to reduce the maximum. Consider second-order: Move δ in feasible direction (sum zero, bounds) changes c_d ≈ ∇_d·δ + 0.5δ^T H_d δ. To reduce max, choose δ negative weighted gradient. This is gradient descent. At optimum, weighted gradient constant on interior coordinates. We can solve by projecting gradient into subspace sum zero: g0 = g - mean(g), step -g0. That's what projected gradient does. We can accelerate with L-BFGS.

Maybe custom L-BFGS on feasible subspace with bounds: maintain step direction p in R^n. At each h, project grad to tangent space of active bounds and sum: For coordinates with active bound (0 or1 at tolerance), gradient component replaced? Simpler projection step.

   a. Smooth SLSQP with gradient at increasing alpha. Or a custom `projected_smooth_optimize`.

Let's design `pair_polish(h,n,deadline, max_delta, min_delta, mode)`:
- Compute c array raw, dx.
- Compute deriv matrix.
- best_c = max(c)*dx.
- deltas = e.g. [0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002]
- For each delta:
   for pass:
      improved=False
      For i in range(n):
        if deadline: break
        For j in range(i+1,n) maybe all if n<=300, else random subset/radius.
        Evaluate directional moves δ=+delta (i +, j -) and -delta.
        Use a function `try_move(i,j,delta)`:
            if bounds not satisfied: continue
            diff = deriv_rows[i] - deriv_rows[j]
            c_try = c + delta*diff
            cross_idx1 = n-1 + j-i; cross_idx2 = n-1 + i-j
            c_try[cross_idx1] += delta*delta
            c_try[cross_idx2] += delta*delta
            m = np.max(c_try)
            if m*dx < best_c - 1e-15:
                accept: update h, c, deriv rows.
            else undo? We didn't modify.
   But evaluating both directions means ±delta; for -delta diff sign flips, cross same.
- After each delta no improvement break.
Accept update:
   h[i] += delta, h[j] -= delta (for direction; ensure bounds)

So gradient function fast:
```
def smooth_obj_grad(x,n,alpha):
    h=np.clip(x,0,1)
    raw=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')  # length L
    v=raw*(2.0/n)
    m=np.max(v)
    a=alpha*(v-m)
    mx=np.max(a)
    if mx>700:
       e=np.exp(a-mx); s=e.sum(); val=m+(mx+np.log(s))/alpha
       w=e/s
    else:
       e=np.exp(a); s=e.sum(); val=m+np.log(s)/alpha; w=e/s
    W=w.astype(float)
    # A
    C1=np.convolve(W, (1-h)[::-1], mode='full')
    A=C1[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    C2=np.convolve(h, W, mode='full')
    B=C2[:n]
    grad= (2.0/n)*(A-B)
    mask=(x>0)&(x<1)
    grad = grad*mask
    return val, grad
```

Build derivative:
```
def build_deriv(h,n):
 L=2*n-1; D=np.zeros((n,L))
 for p in range(n):
   D[p, n-1:n-1+(n-p)] = 1.0 - h[p:]
   D[p, n-1-p:n] += -h[:p+1]
 return D
```
Check indices: first length n-p, start n-1, end n-1+n-p exclusive. For p=0 length n -> indices n-1..2n-2? n-1+n =2n-1 exclusive => max 2n-2, good.
Second: start n-1-p, end n exclusive length p+1. For p=0 start n-1, length1 (index zero). Adds -h[0]. At index n-1, first added 1-h[0], second -h[0] =>1-2h0. Good.

def try_move(i,j,delta,h,c,deriv,n,buff1,buff2):
   # assumes bounds ok and i!=j
   np.subtract(deriv[i], deriv[j], out=buff1)
   buff1 *= delta

   idx2 = n - 1 - d

   buff2[idx2] += dsq

`pair_polish_full(h,n,deadline, deltas, max_passes)`:
- state arrays.
- best_c = max(c)*dx.
- For delta in deltas:
   for pass in range(max_passes):
      improved=False
      for i in range(n):
         if time > deadline: break
         for j in range(i+1,n):
             # direction +
             if h[i]+delta <=1+eps and h[j]-delta >=-eps:
                 m=try_move(..., best_c)
                 if m is not None: best_c=m; improved=True
             # direction -
             if h[i]-delta >=-eps and h[j]+delta <=1+eps:
                 m=try_move(..., delta=-delta)
                 if m is not None: best_c=m; improved=True
      if not improved: break
   if time break
- return h, best_c (c? no need)
This may be slow. For n=250, deltas maybe [0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002]. max_passes 3? The full pair loops with try_move uses `np.subtract` etc. Could be heavy. We can reduce by first using `sampled_polish` for random pairs many, then full small deltas for last n if time.

   Use try_move.
This explores many pairs without O(n^2). It can rerandomize with time. For n=250, 200k iterations * O(250) ~50M ops plus numpy small arrays; maybe 10-20s. Good.

Implementation:
```
def projected_gradient(h,n,alpha,max_iter,deadline):
  h = project_feasible
  f = smooth_value(h)
  lr = 0.05 # scale
  for it:
     val,grad = smooth_obj_grad(h,n,alpha) # h feasible
     # line search: try steps lr * factor
     best = None
     for factor in [1,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625]:
        step = lr*factor
        y = project_feasible(h - step*grad, n)
        fy = compute_value_ex? or smooth value? Use smooth value for alpha.
        if fy < val - 1e-14:
            h=y; val=fy; update best; break
     if no decrease: lr *=0.5; if lr <1e-6 break
  return h, exact c
```
This is simple, no equality constraints separate; projection ensures sum. But logsum nonconvex; projected gradient may be slow. We can use momentum/Adam:
```
m=0; v=0; lr=0.01; for beta...
y=project(h - lr*m/hv)
```
Momentum can help. Deterministic and efficient. We can also use Nesterov? But projection onto sum each iter O(n log n). For n=250, 1000 iters okay.

We can implement a more efficient projection using sorting O(n log n) per iter; bisection 50*n maybe similar.
Pseudo:
```
def project_onto_box_sum(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x,float)
    lo = x.min() - 1.0
    hi = x.max() + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.sum(np.clip(x-mid,0,1))
       if s>target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    nu=(lo+hi)/2
    y=np.clip(x-nu,0,1)
    # adjust remaining with tolerance
    y=finalize_sum(y,target,n)
    return y
```
This projects Euclidean exactly but with many floats. We already have previous project_feasible iterative; could use new.

Let's detail local polish functions.

```
def initialize_local_state(h,n):
   c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
   deriv=build_deriv(h,n)
   diff=np.empty(2*n-1)
   cbuf=np.empty(2*n-1)
   return c,deriv,diff,cbuf
```
No classes.

`evaluate_move(i,j,delta,h,deriv,c,n,diff,cbuf):` returns (m, idx1, idx2, dsq). It assumes bounds ok.
```
def evaluate_move(i,j,delta,h,deriv,c,n,diff,cbuf):
    np.subtract(deriv[i], deriv[j], out=diff)
    diff *= delta
    cbuf[:] = c
    cbuf += diff
    idx1 = n - 1 + (j - i)
    idx2 = n - 1 - (j - i)
    dsq = delta*delta
    cbuf[idx1] += dsq
    cbuf[idx2] += dsq
    return float(np.max(cbuf) * (2.0/n)), idx1, idx2, dsq
```
`apply_move(c,h,deriv,diff, delta, idx1, idx2, dsq, i,j,n):`
```
c += diff
c[idx1] += dsq; c[idx2]+=dsq
h[i] += delta; h[j] -= delta
update_deriv_for_point(deriv,i,delta,n)
update_deriv_for_point(deriv,j,-delta,n)
```
Need if idx1==idx2? Only if j-i=0 impossible. Good.

Potential floating error: c may drift from exact correlate after many moves. We can periodically recompute c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full') and build deriv to correct if local search many. For deterministic with many accepts, drift small but use `if accept_count % 1000 ==0: recompute`.

if bounds... return None

   apply...
   return m
return None

`sampled_polish(h,n,deadline,rng,deltas,iterations=None)`:
- Initialize c,deriv,state, best_c.
- For iteration until deadline:
   i,j random; choose delta from deltas (maybe random sign and magnitude). Or choose a random base and scale feasible.
   For each candidate direction? Use random delta from continuous feasible as SA but only accept if improves (greedy). This is stochastic hill climbing.
   Also try fixed small deltas if random fails? We'll call with decreasing deterministic maybe.
Pseudo:
```
for iter in range(max_iters):
  if time>deadline: break
  # random pair and delta from list * (rng sign)
  i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
  if i==j: continue
  d=deltas[rng.randint(len(deltas))] * (1 if rng.rand()<0.5 else -1)
  # clamp to feasible by reducing magnitude if needed
  if d>0:
      if h[i]+d>1: d=1-h[i]
      if h[j]-d<0: d=h[j]
      if d<=1e-15: continue
  else...
  m=try_move_improve(... d ... best_c)
  if m: best_c=m
```
This tries fixed deltas, not continuous. For final fine, use small deltas. Could also use random continuous but greedy; maybe fixed better to converge. Use both: for first half random continuous step `dag = rng.uniform(0.001,0.02) sign`, for second fixed.

Implementation of loops with local state: We'll allocate c, deriv, diff, cbuf. Function `rebuild_state(h,n)` returns c,deriv,diff,cbuf,best_c.
```
def rebuild_local(h,n):
 c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
 deriv = build_deriv(h,n)
 L=2*n-1; diff=np.empty(L); cbuf=np.empty(L); best_c=float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n)); return...
```

In `deterministic_pair_polish` accept count global. When recompute, use exact. For try_move, `best_c` passed as current exact? We update after accept. If we recompute at intervals, set c,deriv new. But the function receives c,deriv by reference; in Python if reassign inside, caller not see unless return. We can do recompute outside loops. Within a delta pass, no recompute maybe. At end, recompute and continue.

But if we modify local arrays in place and use functions, in Python lists? We'll write loops directly in polish functions rather than nested helper with returns for c? `deterministic_pair_polish` code structure:
```
def deterministic_pair_polish(h,n,deadline,deltas,max_passes,recompute_every=4096):
    c,deriv,diff,cbuf,best_c = rebuild_local(h,n)
    accepts=0
    for delta in deltas:
       passes=0
       while passes<max_passes and time.time()<deadline:
          passes+=1
          improved=False
          for i in range(n):
             for j in range(i+1,n):
                # +
                if h[i]+delta <=1+EPS and h[j]-delta >= -EPS:
                    mc = try_move_improve(i,j,delta,h,c,deriv,diff,cbuf,best_c,[])
                    if mc is not None: best_c=mc; improved=True; accepts+=1
                if h[i]-delta >= -EPS and h[j]+delta <= 1+EPS:
                    mc = try_move_improve(i,j,-delta,...)
                    ...
                if accepts%recompute_every==0: recompute c,deriv,best_c = ... # but local variables update ok
          if not improved: break
       # after delta recompute
       c,deriv,diff,cbuf,best_c = rebuild_local(h,n)
       if bc < best_c? no rebuild exact
    c,deriv,... = rebuild_local(h,n)
    return h,best_c
```
But recompute inside inner might break `diff,cbuf` buffers? If we reassign, the local references in try_move in same pass need new arrays. We can recompute at top of each pass or after delta only. Drift during one full pass with few accepts likely small; skip inside. At each delta recompute.

`try_move_improve`:
```
def try_move_improve(i,j,delta,h,c,deriv,n,diff,cbuf,best_c):
   if delta ==0: return None
   if delta >0:
      if h[i] + delta > 1.0 + 1e-12: return None
      if h[j] - delta < -1e-12: return None
   else:
      ...
   m,idx1,idx2,dsq = evaluate_move(...)
   if m + 1e-15 < best_c:
       apply_move(...)
       return m
   return None
```
Potential issue: After replacing h[i],h[j], if they become -1e-16 due floating, clip in final? We can if small: `if h[i]<0 and h[i]>-1e-12: h[i]=0` etc. Or check with EPS and clamp in apply. In apply, after update:
```
h[i] = float(np.clip(h[i],0,1))
h[j] = float(np.clip(h[j],0,1))
```
But clipping may change sum by tiny; okay. We can add clip and maybe adjust sum occasionally. Local moves preserve sum exactly if no clip. If clip, sum drift; recompute and project. Use EPS 1e-14 and only clamp if within. If clamped, sum drift small; final project.

`evaluate_move` uses c and deriv; if h near bounds, candidate may slightly infeasible due EPS; okay.

SA function with local state:
```
def sa_local(h,n,deadline,rng,T0=0.01,Tend=1e-6,total_est=100000):

```
def project_onto_polytope(x, target):
    x = np.array(x, dtype=float, copy=False)
    # if sum already close and bounds, return adjust
    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(50):
       mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
       y = np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
       s = float(np.sum(y))
       if s > target:
           lo = mid
       else:
           hi = mid
    nu = 0.5*(lo+hi)
    y = np.clip(x - nu, 0.0, 1.0)
    y = finalize_sum(y,target)
    return y
```
But if x is already within bounds and sum close, bisection maybe overkill; we can fast path:
```
if np.all(x>=-eps) and np.all(x<=1+eps) and abs(x.sum()-target)<1e-12: return clip+finalize
```

`finalize_sum(y,target)`:
```
def finalize_sum(y,target):
    y = np.clip(y,0,1)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(y))
    if abs(diff) < 1e-12: return y
    # distribute diff to coordinates with room
    if diff > 0:
        idx = np.where(y < 1.0 - 1e-12)[0]
    else:
        idx = np.where(y > 1e-12)[0]
    if len(idx)==0: return y
    # add equal? But may exceed bounds
    step = diff / len(idx)
    for _ in range(10):
        idx = np.where((diff>0)&(y<1-1e-12) or (diff<0)&(y>1e-12))[0]
        if len(idx)==0: break
        add = diff/len(idx)
        y[idx] += add
        diff = target - float(np.sum(y))
        if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
    # final large index
    diff = target - float(np.sum(y))
    if diff>0:
       idx=np.argmax(1-y)
    else:
       idx=np.argmax(y)
    if diff!=0:
       y[idx] += diff
       y[idx] = np.clip(y[idx],0,1)
    return y
```
But final one might not exactly due sum. Since target feasible, adding to one with room should if room enough. If room not enough due numerical, distribute. Good.

Maybe use previous `project_feasible` iterative which is simpler and preserves feasibility for already feasible. For Adam, we can use `project_onto_polytope`. Let's implement both: `project_feasible` for final (fast iterative); `polytope_projection` for Adam.

def adam_project(h,n,deadline,alpha_list=None):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); f=smooth_value(h,n,alpha?) # but alpha varies
   for alpha in [20,100,400,1500,5000,20000]:
      m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0; lr=0.03
      it=0
      while time < deadline - 5:
         it+=1
         val,grad = smooth_obj_grad(h,n,alpha)
         # to avoid huge values, if grad norm small break
         t+=1
         m=0.9*m+0.1*grad
         v=0.999*v+0.001*grad*grad
         mh=m/(1-0.9**t); vh=v/(1-0.999**t)
         denom=np.sqrt(vh)+1e-8
         accepted=False
         for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625]:
            step = lr*factor
            y = polytope_projection(h - step*mh/denom, n/2.0)
            fy = smooth_value(y,n,alpha)
            if fy <= val + 1e-14? For nonconvex, require fy < val - 1e-15? Use fy < val - 1e-14 maybe too strict; if no decrease, break.
               h=y; val=fy; accepted=True; break
         if not accepted:
            lr *= 0.5
            if lr < 1e-8: break
         # optional if val exact? no
         if time > deadline -100? etc
      h=project_feasible(h,n); c=compute_c_value(h,n); update best
```

```
def build_diag_indices(n):
    idx2 = [None]*n; idx1=[None]*n
    for p in range(n):
       if p>0:
          rows=np.arange(p, dtype=np.int64)
          cols=np.arange(n-1+p, n-1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
          idx2[p]=(rows,cols)
       else: idx2[p]=(None,None)
       if p+1<n:
          rows=np.arange(p+1,n,dtype=np.int64)
          cols=np.arange(n, 2*n-p-1, dtype=np.int64)
          idx1[p]=(rows,cols)
       else: idx1[p]=(None,None)
    return idx2,idx1
```
Then update:
```
def update_deriv_for_point(deriv, p, delta, idx2, idx1):
    deriv[p, n-1] -= 2.0*delta
    rows,cols = idx2[p]
    if rows is not None: deriv[rows, cols] -= delta
    rows,cols = idx1[p]
    if rows is not None: deriv[rows, cols] -= delta
```
No `n` needed except idx0; pass idx0 or len(h)? We'll pass n. Good.

def resample_h(h,n_old,n_new):
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,h)
```

def project_feasible(x,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(x,dtype=float),0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    if abs(float(np.sum(h))-target) < 1e-12:
        return finalize_sum(h,target)
    return polytope_projection(h,target)
```
`polytope_projection` expects x maybe unclipped; it clips internally. It returns float sum close. Good.

`finalize_sum` as above. It should not break bounds. If after final one add bounds broken, we can distribute again. Simpler:
```
def finalize_sum(h,target):
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(20):
        diff=target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
        if diff>0:
            free=np.where(h<1-1e-14)[0]
        else: free=np.where(h>1e-14)[0]
        if free.size==0: break
        add=diff/free.size
        # ensure no bounds
        if diff>0:
           add=min(add, (1-h[free]).min() if free.size else 0)
        else: add=max(add, -h[free].min())
        h[free]+=add
    # remaining exact to one feasible with enough room
    diff=target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff)>0:
       if diff>0: idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); room=1-h[idx]; add=diff if diff<=room else room
       else: idx=int(np.argmax(h)); room=h[idx]; add=-diff if -diff<=room else -room
       h[idx]+=add
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
If after adding one diff still due rounding. Fine.

`polytope_projection` bisection:
```
def polytope_projection(x,target):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    # if x all in bounds and sum close return finalize
    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x)) + 1.0
    temp = np.empty_like(x)
    for _ in range(45):
       nu=0.5*(lo+hi)
       np.clip(x-nu,0,1,out=temp)
       s=float(np.sum(temp))
       if s>target: lo=nu
       else: hi=nu
    nu=0.5*(lo+hi)
    np.clip(x-nu,0,1,out=temp)
    return finalize_sum(temp,target)
```
But if x very infeasible (e.g., all -100), lo=-101, hi maybe? The solution ν may be outside? For box+sum target, ν in [min(x)-1, max(x)+1] indeed. Good.
Fast path if target within sum? But okay.

In `smooth_obj_grad`, if x outside [0,1], h clipped, but penalty uses sum x. The gradient wrt x zero outside due mask. This may cause L-BFGS-B to not move if at bounds? Bounds constrain. Fine.

def lbfgs_call(h,n,alpha,mu,maxiter,deadline):
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    try:
       res = minimize(lbfgs_value, h, jac=lbfgs_grad, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-7,'maxls':30})
       if res.x is not None: return np.asarray(res.x,float)
    except: pass
    return h
```

def run(...):
   t0=...
   deadline=t0+max(10,budget_s-5)
   rng=RandomState(seed)
   # parent
   h,n = get_initial()
   best = (h,c,n)
   # Smooth main
   smooth_deadline = min(deadline - max(150, 0.35*(deadline-t0)), t0 + 350)
   h2,c2=smooth_refine(bh,bn,smooth_deadline,rng,strength=1)
   update
   # Local main
   local_deadline = min(deadline - max(120, 0.25*(deadline-t0)), t0 + 800?)
   h2,c2=local_refine(..., local_deadline, strength=1)
   update
   # Other candidates if time
   if time < deadline - 80:
       candidates = [(int(1.6*n), 'interp'), (int(0.6*n), 'interp')]
       for k,(n_try, typ) in enumerate(candidates):
          if n_try<=4 or n_try>700: continue
          limit = min(deadline - 60, time + 120 if k==0 else 60)
          h_try = resample(best_h,best_n,n_try) if typ else random...
          h_try,c_try = smooth_refine(h_try,n_try, limit, strength=2)
          update
          if time < limit - 20:
             local_l = min(deadline - 40, limit + 60)
             h_try,c_try=local_refine(h_try,n_try, local_l, strength=2)
             update
   # random restart at main n if time
   if time < deadline - 80:
      h_rand = random_initial(best_n, rng)
      limit = min(deadline - 50, time+80)
      h_rand,c_rand=smooth_refine(..., limit, strength=2)
      update
      ... local with strength=2
   # final polish
   if best_h is not None and time < deadline - 3:
       h2,c2=local_refine(best_h,best_n, deadline-0.5, strength=3)
   best = project/recompute
   return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

`smooth_core` details:
```
def smooth_core(x, n, alpha):
    h = np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    dx=2.0/n
    v = raw * dx
    m = float(np.max(v))
    shifted = alpha * (v - m)
    mx = float(np.max(shifted))
    if mx > 700.0:
       e = np.exp(shifted - mx)
    else:
       e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       # fallback uniform? set e max =1
       e.fill(0.0); e[np.argmax(shifted)] = 1.0; s=1.0; val=m
    else:
       if mx > 700.0:
          val = m + (mx + np.log(s)) / alpha
       else:
          val = m + np.log(s) / alpha
    w = e / s
    W = w
    Y = 1.0 - h
    C1 = np.convolve(W, Y[::-1], mode='full')
    A = C1[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    C2 = np.convolve(h, W, mode='full')
    B = C2[n-1:2*n-1]
    grad = dx * (A - B)
    mask = (x > 0.0) & (x < 1.0)
    grad = grad * mask
    return val, grad, w
```

def local_refine(h,n,deadline,rng,strength=1):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute
   remaining = deadline - time.time()
   if remaining <= 5: return best_h,best_c
   # phase 1 greedy
   if strength >= 1:
      if n <= 650:
         greedy_lim = min(deadline, time.time() + remaining*0.35)
         if strength < 3:
            greedy_lim = min(deadline, greedy_lim)
         h,best_c = greedy_polish(h,n,greedy_lim,rng)
   # phase 2 SA
   if strength >= 1:
      sa_lim = min(deadline, time.time() + remaining*0.4)
      if time < sa_lim - 5:
         h,best_c = sa_local(h,n,sa_lim,rng)
   # phase 3 deterministic
   if time < deadline - 2:
      if n <= 280 and strength == 1 and (deadline - time) > 15:
         h,best_c = deterministic_pair_polish(h,n,deadline-0.5)
      elif n <= 600 and (deadline - time) > 10:
         # greedy short
         h,best_c = greedy_polish(h,n,deadline-0.5,rng)
   # final rebuild/project
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c_value(h,n)
   return h,best_c
```

def greedy_polish(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = np.array(h, copy=True)
    c, deriv, diff, cbuf, best_c, diag = init_local_state(h,n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    deltas = [0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001,5e-5,2e-5,1e-5]
    no_imp = 0
    it = 0
    accepts=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 0.5:
       it +=1
       i = rng.randint(n); j = rng.randint(n)
       if i==j: continue
       # choose delta
       if rng.rand() < 0.7:
          d = deltas[rng.randint(len(deltas))]
          if rng.rand()<0.5: d = -d
       else:
          d = np.exp(rng.uniform(np.log(1e-5), np.log(0.03)))
          if rng.rand()<0.5: d=-d
       # try if feasible
       if d > 0:
          maxd = min(1-h[i], h[j])
          if maxd < 1e-14: continue
          if d > maxd: d = maxd
       else:
          maxneg = min(h[i], 1-h[j])
          if maxneg < 1e-14: continue
          if -d > maxneg: d = -maxneg
       if abs(d) < 1e-7: 
          # sometimes use exact feasible midpoint? skip
          continue
       mc = try_move_improve(i,j,d,h,c,deriv,diag,n,diff,cbuf,best_c)
       if mc is not None:
          best_c=mc; no_imp=0; accepts+=1
          if accepts % 4096 == 0:
             c,deriv,diff,cbuf,_,diag = init_local_state(h,n)  # but this reassigns; okay inside loop
             # if exact best lower? exact rebuild best_c maybe
             exact = float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
             if exact < best_c: best_c=exact
              if exact > best_c + 1e-12: # drift? set to exact?
                 best_c=exact
          if best_c < float(np.max(c)*dx)? Wait after init reassign, c is new. We can if we recompute best_h? h unchanged. best_h copy? best_h already at current. If exact improved, update best_h=h.copy().
       else:
          no_imp +=1
          if no_imp > 40000 and it % 1000 ==0: break
    # after loop, maybe deterministic? no
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    cval=compute_c_value(h,n)
    if cval < best_c: best_c=cval; best_h=h.copy()
    elif best_c < compute? Need best_h maybe from earlier; We have best_h. But if h got worse after some rejects, best_h remains. Return best.
    return best_h,best_c
```

Detailed code for `build_deriv_indices` and cache:
```
_DIAG_CACHE = {}
def build_deriv_indices(n):
    idx2 = [None]*n
    idx1 = [None]*n
    for p in range(n):
        if p > 0:
            rows = np.arange(p, dtype=np.int64)
            cols = np.arange(n-1+p, n-1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
            idx2[p] = (rows, cols)
        else:
            idx2[p] = (None, None)
        if p + 1 < n:
            rows = np.arange(p+1, n, dtype=np.int64)
            cols = np.arange(n, 2*n - p - 1, dtype=np.int64)
            idx1[p] = (rows, cols)
        else:
            idx1[p] = (None, None)
    return idx2, idx1
```
Check `cols` for second: `np.arange(n-1+p, n-1, -1, dtype=np.int64)`. If start equals stop? p=0 not. For p>0, start=n+p-1, stop=n-1, includes n..n+p-1 length p. Example n=5,p=2: start6, stop4 => [6,5]. P as second pairs k=0 d=2 idx n? n-1+2=6; k=1 d=1 idx5. Good.
First: `np.arange(n, 2*n - p - 1, dtype=int)`. For p=0,n=5: start5, stop9 => [5,6,7,8] for k=1 d1 idx5; k4 d4 idx8. Good. For p=4: start5, stop5 empty.
Memory: `np.arange(n, 2*n-p-1)` stop could be 2n? p=0 stop2n-1, max index 2n-2, good.

`update_deriv_for_point(deriv, p, delta, idx2, idx1, idx0):`
```
def update_deriv_for_point(deriv,p,delta,idx2,idx1,idx0):
    deriv[p, idx0] -= 2.0*delta
    rows,cols=idx2[p]
    if rows is not None:
        deriv[rows, cols] -= delta
    rows,cols=idx1[p]
    if rows is not None:
        deriv[rows, cols] -= delta
```
Potential issue: If advanced indexing with duplicate indices? For a given p, rows arrays unique; okay. `-= delta` with duplicate not. If idx2 and idx1 arrays are int64, advanced indexing returns copy for get, but `deriv[rows, cols] -= delta` does buffered setitem; if duplicates none. Good.

def init_local_state(h,n):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    deriv = build_deriv(h,n)
    L=2*n-1
    diff=np.empty(L); cbuf=np.empty(L)
    best_c=float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
    idx2, idx1 = get_deriv_indices(n)
    return c,deriv,diff,cbuf,best_c,(idx2,idx1)
```

`evaluate Move`:
```
def evaluate_move(i,j,delta,h,deriv,c,n,diff,cbuf):
    np.subtract(deriv[i], deriv[j], out=diff)
    diff *= delta
    cbuf[:] = c
    cbuf += diff
    idx0 = n - 1
    idx1 = idx0 + (j - i)
    idx2 = idx0 - (j - i)
    dsq = delta*delta
    cbuf[idx0] -= 2.0*dsq
    cbuf[idx1] += dsq
    cbuf[idx2] += dsq
    return float(np.max(cbuf) * (2.0/n), idx0, idx1, idx2, dsq)
```
The function currently returns only float; use tuple:
`return m, idx0, idx1, idx2, dsq`.

`apply_move_state`:
```
def apply_move_state(c,h,deriv,diag,i,j,delta,diff,dsq,idx0,idx1,idx2):
    c += diff
    c[idx0] -= 2.0*dsq
    c[idx1] += dsq
    c[idx2] += dsq
    h_i = h[i] + delta; h_j = h[j] - delta
    if h_i < 0.0: h_i=0.0
    elif h_i > 1.0: h_i=1.0
    if h_j < 0.0: h_j=0.0
    elif h_j > 1.0: h_j=1.0
    h[i]=h_i; h[j]=h_j
    idx2_list, idx1_list = diag
    update_deriv_for_point(deriv, i, delta, idx2_list, idx1_list, n-1) # need n? idx0 passed
    update_deriv_for_point(deriv, j, -delta, idx2_list, idx1_list, n-1)
```
Need pass idx0 to update. `apply_state` can get `n`? It has idx0. Pass idx0 to update. Good.
But if we clip h_i/h_j due eps, derivative update assumes exact delta. If clipped significantly (shouldn't due bounds check), inconsistency. Bounds check in try_move prevents > eps. In SA, we compute feasible low/high exactly, so no clip. Fine.

def try_move_improve(i,j,delta,h,c,deriv,diag,n,diff,cbuf,best_c):
    if delta > 0.0:
        if h[i] + delta > 1.0 + 1e-12 or h[j] - delta < -1e-12: return None
    elif delta < 0.0:
        if h[i] + delta < -1e-12 or h[j] - delta > 1.0 + 1e-12: return None
    else: return None
    m, idx0, idx1, idx2, dsq = evaluate_move(...)
    if m + 1e-15 < best_c:
        apply_move_state(c,h,deriv,diag,i,j,delta,diff,dsq,idx0,idx1,idx2)
        return m
    return None
```

`finalize_sum`: Must ensure final returns sum exactly enough. Let's implement robust:
```
def finalize_sum(h, target):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(10):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            idx = np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-12)[0]
        else:
            idx = np.where(h > 1e-12)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            break
        add = diff / idx.size
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h[idx]
            amax = room.min()
            if add > amax: add = amax
        else:
            amax = h[idx].min()
            if add < -amax: add = -amax
        h[idx] += add
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 0.0:
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            room = 1.0 - h[idx]
            add = diff if diff <= room else room
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            room = h[idx]
            add = -diff if -diff <= room else -room
        h[idx] += add
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
If after final add diff still not zero due room insufficient? Since target feasible, there should be enough total room; if amax insufficient, loop continues. Fine.

`smooth_refine` code:
```
def smooth_refine(h,n,deadline,strength):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute
    if strength == 1:
       alphas=[50,200,800,3000,10000]
       alphas_sls=[2000,8000,30000]
    elif strength ==2: alphas=[100,500,2000]; sls=[]
    else: alphas=[200,800]; sls=[]
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time>deadline-8: break
       mus=[0.5,5.0] if alpha<500 else [5.0,50.0,200.0] if strength==1 else [5.0,50.0]
       for mu in mus:
          if time>deadline-8: break

def sa_local(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h=np.array(h,copy=True); h=project
    c,deriv,diff,cbuf,current_c,diag=init_local_state(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=current_c
    rem=deadline-time; total_est=int(max(20000, min(300000, rem*1000)))
    it=0; accepts=0
    T0=0.004; Tend=1e-7
    while time < deadline-0.5:
       it+=1
       i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
       if i==j: continue
       low = max(-h[i], h[j]-1.0)
       high = min(1-h[i], h[j])
       if high <= low + 1e-14: continue
       delta = low + rng.random()*(high-low)
       if abs(delta) < 1e-7: continue
       m,idx0,idx1,idx2,dsq=evaluate_move(...)
       if not finite: continue
       if m < current_c:
           accept=True
       else:
           frac=it/total_est; if frac>1: frac=1
           T=T0*(Tend/T0)**frac
           if T <= 0: accept=False
           else: accept = rng.random() < np.exp((current_c-m)/T)
       if accept:
           apply...
           current_c=m
           accepts+=1
           if m < best_c:
               best_c=m; best_h=h.copy()
           if accepts % 8192 ==0:
               c,deriv,diff,cbuf,cur_true,diag=init_local_state(h,n)
               if cur_true < best_c: best_c=cur_true; best_h=h.copy()
               current_c=cur_true
       if it % 10000 ==0 and current_c > best_c + 0.02:
           h=best_h.copy(); c,deriv,diff,cbuf,current_c,diag=init_local_state(h,n)
    # final compare current h and best
```

We can precompute lazily:
```
def build_aff_indices(n):
   aff=[None]*n
   for p in range(n):
      rows = np.empty(2*(n-1), dtype=np.int64)
      cols = np.empty(2*(n-1), dtype=np.int64)
      k=0
      for q in range(n):
         if q==p: continue
         rows[k]=q; cols[k]=n-1+(q-p); k+=1
         rows[k]=q; cols[k]=n-1+(p-q); k+=1
      aff[p]=(rows,cols)
   return aff
```
For n=600, building O(n^2) Python loops 360k, okay but per p inner n loop ->360k, fine. Memory rows/cols 2*599*600 ~718k ints each.

def update_deriv_for_point(deriv,p,delta,aff,idx0):
   deriv[p, idx0] -= 2*delta
   rows,cols=aff[p]
   if rows is not None and rows.size:
      deriv[rows, cols] -= delta
```
This is O(n) vectorized. Great.

def build_aff_indices(n):
    aff=[]
    idx0=n-1
    for p in range(n):
       rows=np.empty(2*(n-1), dtype=np.int64)
       cols=np.empty(2*(n-1), dtype=np.int64)
       k=0
       for q in range(n):
          if q==p: continue
          rows[k]=q; cols[k]=idx0 + (q-p); k+=1
          rows[k]=q; cols[k]=idx0 + (p-q); k+=1
       aff.append((rows,cols))
    return aff
```

Now build_deriv actual code:
```
def build_deriv(h,n):
    L=2*n-1
    D=np.zeros((n,L), dtype=float)
    rev1 = 1.0 - h[::-1]
    negh = -h
    for p in range(n):
        D[p, p : n+p] += rev1
        D[p, n-1-p : 2*n-1-p] += negh
    return D
```
Check slice end for second: `2*n-1-p`. For p=n-1: start0, end 2n-1-(n-1)=n, length n? L=2n-1, indices0..n-1 length n. Good (d=-p..n-1-p = -(n-1)..0). For p=0: start n-1, end 2n-1, length n indices n-1..2n-2. Good. First slice p:n+p length n, max p=n-1 -> n-1:2n-1 length n indices n-1..2n-2. At p=n-1, first and second both cover idx n-1..2n-2? Sum target. Good.

Sine: `0.5+0.5*np.sin(2π*(freq*arange/n+phase))`; project.
Uniform noise: `0.5 + 0.5*(2*rng.rand(n)-1)`; sum ~n/2; project.

Now, `resample_h`: If n_old==n_new return copy. Use midpoints.

Now, `run`: initial if `initial_h_values` length maybe 0; catch. If parent sum far, project. Maybe parent n large 250. Good.

Potential issue: The previous parent may have n=250 and values optimized for previous algorithm; project_feasible using polytope may change it (Euclidean projection) more than previous iterative? If sum already exact and bounds, fast path returns unchanged (clip/finalize tiny). Good.

Now, final code. Let's output.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_DIAG_CACHE = {}
_GREEDY_DELTAS = (
    0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.0002,
    0.0001, 5e-5, 2e-5, 1e-5
)


def compute_c_value(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(raw)) * dx


def finalize_sum(h, target):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(12):
        diff = float(target - np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-12)[0]
        else:
            idx = np.where(h > 1e-12)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            break
        add = diff / float(idx.size)
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = (1.0 - h[idx]).min()
            if add > room:
                add = room
        else:
            room = h[idx].min()
            if add < -room:
                add = -room
        if add == 0.0:
            break
        h[idx] += add

    diff = float(target - np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 0.0:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(float(diff), float(1.0 - h[idx]))
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            add = max(float(diff), -float(h[idx]))
        h[idx] += add
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def polytope_projection(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    y = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(float(np.sum(y)) - float(target)) < 1e-13:
        return finalize_sum(y, target)

    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x)) + 1.0
    tmp = np.empty_like(x, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(45):
        nu = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        np.clip(x - nu, 0.0, 1.0, out=tmp)
        s = float(np.sum(tmp))
        if s > target:
            lo = nu
        else:
            hi = nu
    nu = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    np.clip(x - nu, 0.0, 1.0, out=tmp)
    return finalize_sum(tmp, target)


def project_feasible(x, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    return polytope_projection(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), target)


def smooth_value(x, n, alpha):
    h = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    v = raw * (2.0 / float(n))
    m = float(np.max(v))
    shifted = alpha * (v - m)
    mx = float(np.max(shifted))
    if mx > 700.0:
        e = np.exp(shifted - mx)
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return m
        return float(m + (mx + np.log(s)) / alpha)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return m
    return float(m + np.log(s) / alpha)


def smooth_grad(x, n, alpha):
    h = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    raw = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    v = raw * (2.0 / float(n))
    m = float(np.max(v))
    shifted = alpha * (v - m)
    mx = float(np.max(shifted))
    if mx > 700.0:
        e = np.exp(shifted - mx)
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            val = m
            w = np.zeros_like(v)
            w[int(np.argmax(v))] = 1.0
        else:
            val = m + (mx + np.log(s)) / alpha
            w = e / s
    else:
        e = np.exp(shifted)
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            val = m
            w = np.zeros_like(v)
            w[int(np.argmax(v))] = 1.0
        else:
            val = m + np.log(s) / alpha
            w = e / s

    W = w.astype(float)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    # Gradient for np.correlate(h, 1-h) convention.
    y = 1.0 - h
    C1 = np.convolve(y, W, mode='full')
    F = C1[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

    C2 = np.convolve(W, h[::-1], mode='full')
    H = C2[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]

    grad = (2.0 / float(n)) * (F - H)
    mask = (x > 0.0) & (x < 1.0)
    grad = grad * mask
    val = float(val)
    return val, grad


def smooth_value_only(x, n, alpha):
    return smooth_value(x, n, alpha)


def smooth_grad_only(x, n, alpha):
    _, g = smooth_grad(x, n, alpha)
    return g


def lb_value(x, n, alpha, mu):
    base = smooth_value(x, n, alpha)
    s = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)
    return float(base + 0.5 * mu * s * s)


def lb_grad(x, n, alpha, mu):
    _, g = smooth_grad(x, n, alpha)
    s = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)
    return g + mu * s


def sl_value(x, n, alpha):
    return smooth_value(x, n, alpha)


def sl_grad(x, n, alpha):
    _, g = smooth_grad(x, n, alpha)
    return g


def sum_constraint(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)


def sum_constraint_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=float)


def lb_call(h, n, alpha, mu, maxiter):
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lb_value, h, args=(n, alpha, mu), jac=lb_grad,
            method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
            options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-14, 'gtol': 1e-8, 'maxls': 30}
        )
        if getattr(res, 'x', None) is not None:
            return np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return h.copy()


def sl_call(h, n, alpha, maxiter):
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    constraints = [
        {'type': 'eq', 'fun': sum_constraint, 'jac': sum_constraint_jac, 'args': (n,)}
    ]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            sl_value, h, args=(n, alpha), jac=sl_grad,
            method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=constraints,
            options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-14}
        )
        if getattr(res, 'x', None) is not None:
            return np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return h.copy()


def smooth_refine(h, n, deadline, strength):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c_value(best_h, n)

    if strength == 1:
        schedule = [
            (50.0, 1.0), (200.0, 1.0), (600.0, 5.0),
            (2000.0, 20.0), (8000.0, 120.0), (30000.0, 500.0)
        ]
        sl_alphas = [2000.0, 10000.0]
    elif strength == 2:
        schedule = [
            (150.0, 3.0), (800.0, 15.0), (5000.0, 80.0)
        ]
        sl_alphas = []
    else:
        schedule = [(400.0, 10.0), (3000.0, 60.0)]
        sl_alphas = []

    if n < 250:
        maxiter = 90
    elif n < 450:
        maxiter = 55
    else:
        maxiter = 30

    for alpha, mu in schedule:
        if time.time() > deadline - 8.0:
            break
        h2 = project_feasible(lb_call(h, n, alpha, mu, maxiter), n)
        c2 = compute_c_value(h2, n)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()
            h = h2.copy()

    if strength == 1 and n <= 450:
        for alpha in sl_alphas:
            if time.time() > deadline - 12.0:
                break
            h2 = project_feasible(sl_call(h, n, alpha, 70), n)
            c2 = compute_c_value(h2, n)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()
                h = h2.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = compute_c_value(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c


def build_deriv(h, n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    D = np.zeros((n, L), dtype=float)
    rev_val = 1.0 - h[::-1]
    neg_h = -h
    for p in range(n):
        D[p, p:n + p] += rev_val
        D[p, n - 1 - p:2 * n - 1 - p] += neg_h
    return D


def build_aff_indices(n):
    aff = []
    idx0 = n - 1
    m = 2 * (n - 1)
    for p in range(n):
        rows = np.empty(m, dtype=np.intp)
        cols = np.empty(m, dtype=np.intp)
        k = 0
        for q in range(n):
            if q == p:
                continue
            rows[k] = q
            cols[k] = idx0 + (q - p)
            k += 1
            rows[k] = q
            cols[k] = idx0 + (p - q)
            k += 1
        aff.append((rows, cols))
    return aff


def get_aff_indices(n):
    got = _DIAG_CACHE.get(n)
    if got is None:
        got = build_aff_indices(n)
        _DIAG_CACHE[n] = got
    return got


def update_deriv_for_point(deriv, p, delta, aff, idx0):
    deriv[p, idx0] -= 2.0 * delta
    rows, cols = aff[p]
    if rows is not None and rows.size:
        deriv[rows, cols] -= delta


def init_local_state(h, n):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    deriv = build_deriv(h, n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    diff = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    cbuf = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    best_c = float(np.max(c)) * (2.0 / float(n))
    aff = get_aff_indices(n)
    return c, deriv, diff, cbuf, best_c, aff


def evaluate_move(i, j, delta, h, deriv, c, n, diff, cbuf):
    np.subtract(deriv[i], deriv[j], out=diff)
    diff *= delta
    cbuf[:] = c
    cbuf += diff

    idx0 = n - 1
    d = j - i
    idx1 = idx0 + d
    idx2 = idx0 - d
    dsq = delta * delta

    cbuf[idx0] -= 2.0 * dsq
    cbuf[idx1] += dsq
    cbuf[idx2] += dsq
    return float(np.max(cbuf) * (2.0 / float(n))), idx0, idx1, idx2, dsq


def apply_move_state(c, h, deriv, aff, i, j, delta, diff, dsq, idx0, idx1, idx2):
    c += diff
    c[idx0] -= 2.0 * dsq
    c[idx1] += dsq
    c[idx2] += dsq

    hi = h[i] + delta
    hj = h[j] - delta
    if hi < 0.0:
        hi = 0.0
    elif hi > 1.0:
        hi = 1.0
    if hj < 0.0:
        hj = 0.0
    elif hj > 1.0:
        hj = 1.0
    h[i] = hi
    h[j] = hj

    idx0 = n_minus_1(h)
    update_deriv_for_point(deriv, i, delta, aff, idx0)
    update_deriv_for_point(deriv, j, -delta, aff, idx0)


def n_minus_1(h):
    return len(h) - 1


def try_move_improve(i, j, delta, h, c, deriv, aff, n, diff, cbuf, best_c):
    eps = 1e-12
    if delta > 0.0:
        if h[i] + delta > 1.0 + eps or h[j] - delta < -eps:
            return None
    elif delta < 0.0:
        if h[i] + delta < -eps or h[j] - delta > 1.0 + eps:
            return None
    else:
        return None

    m, idx0, idx1, idx2, dsq = evaluate_move(i, j, delta, h, deriv, c, n, diff, cbuf)
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return None
    if m + 1e-15 < best_c:
        apply_move_state(c, h, deriv, aff, i, j, delta, diff, dsq, idx0, idx1, idx2)
        return m
    return None


def greedy_polish(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True), n)
    c, deriv, diff, cbuf, best_c, aff = init_local_state(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()

    it = 0
    accepts = 0
    no_improve = 0
    stall_limit = 25000 if n <= 320 else 45000

    while time.time() < deadline - 0.5:
        it += 1
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i == j:
            continue

        if rng.rand() < 0.70:
            d = _GREEDY_DELTAS[int(rng.randint(len(_GREEDY_DELTAS)))]
            if rng.rand() < 0.5:
                d = -d
        else:
            d = float(np.exp(rng.uniform(np.log(1e-5), np.log(0.03))))
            if rng.rand() < 0.5:
                d = -d

        if d > 0.0:
            maxd = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
            if maxd < 1e-14:
                continue
            if d > maxd:
                d = maxd
        else:
            maxd = min(h[i], 1.0 - h[j])
            if maxd < 1e-14:
                continue
            if -d > maxd:
                d = -maxd

        if abs(d) < 1e-8:
            continue

        mc = try_move_improve(i, j, d, h, c, deriv, aff, n, diff, cbuf, best_c)
        if mc is not None:
            best_c = mc
            best_h = h.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            accepts += 1
            if accepts % 4096 == 0:
                c, deriv, diff, cbuf, cur_true, aff = init_local_state(h, n)
                if cur_true < best_c:
                    best_c = cur_true
                    best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > stall_limit and it % 1000 == 0:
                break

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    bc = compute_c_value(best_h, n)
    cur_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    cc = compute_c_value(cur_h, n)
    if cc < bc:
        return cur_h, cc
    return best_h, bc


def deterministic_pair_polish(h, n, deadline):
    if n > 320:
        return h, compute_c_value(h, n)

    h = project_feasible(np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True), n)
    c, deriv, diff, cbuf, best_c, aff = init_local_state(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    accepts = 0

    if n <= 180:
        deltas = [0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.0002, 0.0001, 5e-5]
        max_passes = 4
    else:
        deltas = [0.004, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.0002, 0.0001]
        max_passes = 2

    for delta in deltas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 1.0:
            break
        for _pass in range(max_passes):
            if time.time() > deadline - 0.5:
                return best_h, best_c
            improved = False
            for i in range(n):
                if time.time() > deadline - 0.5:
                    break
                for j in range(i + 1, n):
                    if j % 64 == 0 and time.time() > deadline - 0.5:
                        break

                    if h[i] + delta <= 1.0 + 1e-12 and h[j] - delta >= -1e-12:
                        mc = try_move_improve(i, j, delta, h, c, deriv, aff, n, diff, cbuf, best_c)
                        if mc is not None:
                            best_c = mc
                            best_h = h.copy()
                            improved = True
                            accepts += 1
                            if accepts % 2048 == 0:
                                c, deriv, diff, cbuf, cur_true, aff = init_local_state(h, n)
                                if cur_true < best_c:
                                    best_c = cur_true
                                    best_h = h.copy()

                    if h[i] - delta >= -1e-12 and h[j] + delta <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
                        mc = try_move_improve(i, j, -delta, h, c, deriv, aff, n, diff, cbuf, best_c)
                        if mc is not None:
                            best_c = mc
                            best_h = h.copy()
                            improved = True
                            accepts += 1
                            if accepts % 2048 == 0:
                                c, deriv, diff, cbuf, cur_true, aff = init_local_state(h, n)
                                if cur_true < best_c:
                                    best_c = cur_true
                                    best_h = h.copy()
            if not improved:
                break

        c, deriv, diff, cbuf, cur_true, aff = init_local_state(h, n)
        if cur_true < best_c:
            best_c = cur_true
            best_h = h.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    bc = compute_c_value(best_h, n)
    cur_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    cc = compute_c_value(cur_h, n)
    if cc < bc:
        return cur_h, cc
    return best_h, bc


def sa_local(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True), n)
    c, deriv, diff, cbuf, current_c, aff = init_local_state(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = current_c

    rem = max(1.0, deadline - time.time())
    total_est = int(max(20000, min(300000, rem * 1000)))
    T0 = 0.004
    Tend = 1e-7
    it = 0
    accepts = 0

    while time.time() < deadline - 0.5:
        it += 1
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i == j:
            continue

        low = max(-h[i], h[j] - 1.0)
        high = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
        if high <= low + 1e-14:
            continue

        delta = float(low + rng.random() * (high - low))
        if abs(delta) < 1e-7:
            continue

        m, idx0, idx1, idx2, dsq = evaluate_move(i, j, delta, h, deriv, c, n, diff, cbuf)
        if not np.isfinite(m):
            continue

        if m < current_c:
            accept = True
        else:
            frac = float(it) / float(total_est)
            if frac > 1.0:
                frac = 1.0
            T = T0 * (Tend / T0) ** frac
            if T <= 0.0:
                accept = False
            else:
                ratio = (current_c - m) / T
                if ratio > 50.0:
                    accept = True
                else:
                    accept = bool(rng.random() < float(np.exp(ratio)))

        if accept:
            apply_move_state(c, h, deriv, aff, i, j, delta, diff, dsq, idx0, idx1, idx2)
            current_c = m
            accepts += 1
            if m < best_c:
                best_c = m
                best_h = h.copy()

            if accepts % 8192 == 0:
                c, deriv, diff, cbuf, cur_true, aff = init_local_state(h, n)
                if cur_true < best_c:
                    best_c = cur_true
                    best_h = h.copy()
                current_c = cur_true

        if it % 10000 == 0 and current_c > best_c + 0.02:
            h = best_h.copy()
            c, deriv, diff, cbuf, current_c, aff = init_local_state(h, n)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    bc = compute_c_value(best_h, n)
    cur_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    cc = compute_c_value(cur_h, n)
    if cc < bc:
        return cur_h, cc
    return best_h, bc


def local_refine(h, n, deadline, rng, strength):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True), n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c_value(h, n)
    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem <= 4.0 or n <= 2:
        return best_h, best_c

    if strength == 1:
        g_lim = time.time() + min(rem * 0.35, 260.0)
        sa_lim_frac = 0.55
        sa_cap = 320.0
    elif strength == 2:
        g_lim = time.time() + min(rem * 0.55, 70.0)
        sa_lim_frac = 0.45
        sa_cap = 70.0
    else:
        g_lim = deadline - 0.3
        sa_lim_frac = 0.0
        sa_cap = 0.0

    g_lim = min(g_lim, deadline - 1.0)
    if g_lim > time.time() + 3.0 and n <= 750:
        h, best_c = greedy_polish(h, n, g_lim, rng)

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem >= 8.0 and n <= 750:
        sa_lim = time.time() + min(rem * sa_lim_frac, sa_cap)
        if sa_lim > time.time() + 5.0:
            h, best_c = sa_local(h, n, min(sa_lim, deadline - 1.0), rng)

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if strength == 1 and n <= 320 and rem > 18.0:
        h, best_c = deterministic_pair_polish(h, n, deadline - 0.4)
    elif strength == 2 and n <= 480 and rem > 8.0:
        h, best_c = greedy_polish(h, n, deadline - 0.4, rng)

    best_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    bc = compute_c_value(best_h, n)
    cur_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    cc = compute_c_value(cur_h, n)
    if cc < bc:
        return cur_h, cc
    return best_h, bc


def resample_h(h, n_old, n_new):
    if n_old == n_new:
        return np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    x_old = (np.arange(n_old, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_old))
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_new))
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)


def random_initial(n, rng):
    kind = int(rng.randint(4))
    if kind == 0:
        phi = 0.6180339887498949
        frac = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) * phi) % 1.0
        h = (frac < 0.5).astype(float)
    elif kind == 1:
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        h[:n // 2] = 1.0
        if n % 2 == 1:
            h[n // 2] = 0.5
        rng.shuffle(h)
    elif kind == 2:
        freq = int(rng.randint(2, 9))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
        h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (freq * t + phase))
    else:
        h = 0.5 + 0.5 * (2.0 * rng.rand(n) - 1.0)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget < 10.0:
        budget = 1100.0
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float('inf')
    best_n = 0

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = len(h0)
        if n0 < 4:
            raise ValueError('bad initial length')
        best_h = project_feasible(h0, n0)
        best_c = compute_c_value(best_h, n0)
        best_n = n0
    except Exception:
        best_n = 200
        best_h = random_initial(best_n, rng)
        best_c = compute_c_value(best_h, best_n)

    total_budget = max(30.0, deadline - t0)

    # Main refinement at the parent resolution.
    reserve_other = min(260.0, max(90.0, 0.22 * total_budget))
    smooth_deadline = min(
        t0 + min(330.0, 0.35 * total_budget),
        deadline - reserve_other
    )
    if smooth_deadline > t0 + 8.0 and best_h is not None:
        h2, c2 = smooth_refine(best_h, best_n, smooth_deadline, strength=1)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, len(h2)

    local_deadline = min(deadline - reserve_other, deadline)
    if local_deadline > time.time() + 5.0 and best_h is not None:
        h2, c2 = local_refine(best_h, best_n, local_deadline, rng, strength=1)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, len(h2)

    # Try related resolutions.
    if best_h is not None and deadline - time.time() > 80.0:
        cand_ns = []
        for m in (
            int(round(1.6 * best_n)),
            int(round(0.6 * best_n)),
            int(round(0.4 * best_n)),
            420,
            650,
        ):
            m = max(24, int(m))
            if m == best_n or m in cand_ns or m > 850:
                continue
            cand_ns.append(m)
            if len(cand_ns) >= 3:
                break

        if cand_ns and deadline - time.time() > 60.0:
            cand_budget = max(25.0, (deadline - time.time() - 35.0) / float(len(cand_ns)))
            for nn in cand_ns:
                if time.time() > deadline - 45.0:
                    break
                limit1 = min(deadline - 70.0, time.time() + cand_budget)
                if limit1 < time.time() + 12.0:
                    break
                h_try = resample_h(best_h, best_n, nn)
                h_try = project_feasible(h_try, nn)
                h_try, c_try = smooth_refine(h_try, nn, min(limit1, deadline - 60.0), strength=2)
                if c_try < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = h_try, c_try, nn

                limit2 = min(deadline - 30.0, time.time() + max(25.0, cand_budget * 0.65))
                if limit2 > time.time() + 8.0:
                    h_try, c_try = local_refine(h_try, nn, min(limit2, deadline - 20.0), rng, strength=2)
                    if c_try < best_c:
                        best_h, best_c, best_n = h_try, c_try, nn

    # One independent restart at the current best resolution.
    if best_h is not None and deadline - time.time() > 70.0:
        nn = min(max(120, best_n), 520)
        h_rand = random_initial(nn, rng)
        limit1 = min(deadline - 45.0, time.time() + 35.0)
        if limit1 > time.time() + 8.0:
            h_rand, c_rand = smooth_refine(h_rand, nn, limit1, strength=2)
            if c_rand < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h_rand, c_rand, nn
            limit2 = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + 22.0)
            if limit2 > time.time() + 6.0:
                h_rand, c_rand = local_refine(h_rand, nn, limit2, rng, strength=2)
                if c_rand < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = h_rand, c_rand, nn

    # Final polish.
    if best_h is not None and deadline - time.time() > 3.0:
        h_final, c_final = local_refine(best_h, best_n, deadline - 0.3, rng, strength=3)
        if c_final < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h_final, c_final, len(h_final)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c_value(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, int(best_n)
```
